# Comprehensive cinema reference and evidence audit

Latest follow-up: [further technical research](cinema-technical-deep-research.md) adds four individual sala seated counts from event listings, two ambiguous grouped counts, Capri’s digital-conversion date and 19 distinct new photo references. Current totals: 224 room records, 205 practical facts, 183 price observations, 182 image references and 496 sources. [Photo audit](cinema-technical-deep-photos.md) · [Validation](cinema-technical-deep-log.json). Earlier pass narratives follow.

Research snapshot: **19 November 2026**. All **38 current directory entries** have profiles, within a broader catalogue of **59 researched venues**. This consolidates information collected so far; it does **not** claim that every public fact or every sala has been found.

This pass records **58 additional sourced observations across 19 venues**, and links **14 existing chain policies to 51 additional venue/fact combinations**. It adds **16 source records** and unifies source registries from earlier reports. The combined file contains **224 room records**, **205 practical fact records**, **183 price observations**, **182 image/drawing references** and **163 bounded public-review observations**.

[Complete structured reference](cinema-comprehensive-research.json) · [Directory coverage](cinema-directory-coverage.json) · [Validation and access log](cinema-comprehensive-research-log.json) · [Earlier research guide](cinema-dossier.md)

## What this pass adds

- Verdi’s declared website company, contacts, accessibility wording and ticket conditions; separate Verdi Park opening history.
- Exact wheelchair locations and an explicit absence of an induction loop at Zumzeig; access routes, reserved places and hearing support at CCCB; museum-specific services at MACBA.
- Further visitor information for Girona, Renoir and Texas; dated purchase rules and hire options for La Calàndria.
- Opening chronology for Cinesa Diagonal, Heron City/SOM, CASA SEAT and Can Carol; further Foment and Imperial history. A contemporary municipal announcement strengthens Edison’s 2018 opening evidence while preserving the conflicting 2019 description.
- OCINE’s website legal entity and group contact; a separately labelled secondary company association for Imperial.

These observations include more precise extraction and corroboration from previously known pages. Chain-policy links reuse earlier research; they are not 51 new local inspections. No new photographs or reviews were collected in this pass.

## Reading the evidence

“Evidence present” means a qualifying observation exists in the collected records. It does not mean the topic is complete, current, established for every sala or free of conflicting sources. The JSON retains evidence paths and caveats. No completeness percentage is calculated.

- Website holder, operating brand, local lessee, parent group and property owner are separate roles.
- Prices include dated ticket tariffs, memberships and hire fees. Museum entry does not establish screening admission.
- Capacity includes published figures, booking layouts, historical claims and movable-chair configurations. Complex totals are never distributed among rooms.
- Screen, room, stage and floor-area measurements remain separate. Some screen pairs have inferred axes, a disputed value or an LED-display classification.
- Images include exterior and historical material. A venue image is not a verified current photo of every sala; reuse rights are not established.
- Reviews include bounded excerpts/paraphrases and labelled republisher material; they are not complete Google or Tripadvisor exports.

## Main research gaps

1. Complete room inventories and current seat totals, including the treatment of wheelchair positions.
2. Screen sizes and full room length/width/height. No complete room-dimension triple is established.
3. Venue-specific legal operators, leaseholders and current property ownership.
4. Room-specific access routes, hearing loops, accessible subtitles/audio description and sensory sessions.
5. Contacts, hours, current prices and booking/admission rules at venues without published detail.
6. Projector/server/lens and sound models, installation dates, seating plans and room-identified interior photos.

Blocked resources and old pages are recorded in the access log. Some details may need unpublished technical sheets or a venue response; no venue was contacted.

(published) [Phenomena — purchase conditions](https://phenomena-experience.com/index?pag=terminos-de-compra)
- **Booking:** Published terms reject ordinary changes/refunds. If the cinema cancels for technical or force-majeure reasons, it allows a refund request within 14 days. (published) [Phenomena — purchase conditions](https://phenomena-experience.com/index?pag=terminos-de-compra)
- **Pricing conditions:** The purchase terms mention under-12 and over-65 discounts and Club tickets, with official photo ID required. Discount amounts are not specified. (published) [Phenomena — purchase conditions](https://phenomena-experience.com/index?pag=terminos-de-compra)

### Salas and technical evidence

**Sala 1** — phenomena:1; identity: original room catalogue.

- Capacity: 420; Post-renovation seats reported by technical visitor; reported. [Sala Abierta: first-hand Phenomena technical visit](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=2496)
- Capacity: 419; Capacity field in indexed sessions; not independently certified capacity; programme_snapshot. 
- Screen/display: 15.0 × 6.2 m; explicitly stated. [Christie: Phenomena Experience renovation](https://www.christiedigital.com/press-releases/phenomena-experience/)
- Alternative screen observations: [{"width_m": 15.0, "height_m": 6.27, "source_ids": ["phenomena-visit"], "status": "reported"}]
- Room dimensions L/W/H: None / None / None m; floor area None m². Null means not established. 
- **Projection:** Christie CP4435-RGB · 4K RGB laser (published) [Christie: Phenomena Experience renovation](https://www.christiedigital.com/press-releases/phenomena-experience/)
- **Projection:** 35 mm and 70 mm film projection retained (published) [Christie: Phenomena Experience renovation](https://www.christiedigital.com/press-releases/phenomena-experience/)
- **Projection:** Cinemeccanica Victoria 8; UXL-5000HR 5,000 W xenon lamp (reported) [Sala Abierta: first-hand Phenomena technical visit](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=2496)
- **Sound:** Dolby Atmos; CP850 digital and CP650 film processors (reported) [Sala Abierta: first-hand Phenomena technical visit](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=2496)
- **Sound:** 5 Dolby 136 screen systems; 10 SB218XL screen subwoofers; 4 surround subwoofers (reported) [Sala Abierta: first-hand Phenomena technical visit](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=2496)
- **Sound:** 28 Dolby DSR1260/1280 surrounds: 10 side, 8 rear, 10 ceiling (reported) [Sala Abierta: first-hand Phenomena technical visit](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=2496)
- **Sound:** 15 QSC amplifiers; Q-SYS Core 110f; AES67 audio network (reported) [Sala Abierta: first-hand Phenomena technical visit](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=2496)
- **Seating:** Ezcaray Seating; unnumbered seating (reported) [Sala Abierta: first-hand Phenomena technical visit](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=2496)
- Caveat: The current programme reports 419; the 2026 visit reports 420. Older 450-seat descriptions predate the renovation.
- Caveat: Screen material is reported as microperforated in the technical visit.
- Caveat: Model power/brightness ratings are not auditorium measurements.

### Images, reviews and gaps

**5 image/drawing references** and **28 review observations**, linked by ID in the [combined JSON](cinema-comprehensive-research.json).

- Building owner and ultimate shareholders.
- Current wheelchair-space inventory, hearing assistance and step-free route.
- Full review corpus and original review/photo permalinks.
- Exact room dimensions; 419 programme capacity versus 420 reported seats remains unresolved.
- 2026 renovation changes equipment and seating; old technical sheets may be stale.

**No qualifying record in this audit:** Ultimate ownership; Property and tenure evidence; Architecture; Venue/box-office hours; Physical accessibility; Room wheelchair positions; Hearing, visual or cognitive access; Room length × width × height; Room floor area.

### Images, reviews and gaps

**22 image/drawing references** and **28 review observations**, linked by ID in the [combined JSON](cinema-comprehensive-research.json).

- Building owner and venue-specific leaseholding entity.
- Full review corpus and original review/photo permalinks.
- Measured screen and auditorium dimensions remain incomplete; see room catalogue.
- Ticket booking fees, Sunday general tariff, concessions menu and remaining room-specific access/hearing details; published tariffs are now linked.
- Only rooms 1, 6, 8 and 9 are explicitly identified as laser in the operator description. Only rooms 8 and 9 are explicitly identified as Atmos.
- The Infinity Vision page mentions Balmes without identifying a room; no room-level certification inferred.
- March 2026 operator interview describes the circuit as digital, with phased laser/Atmos upgrades and no active 35/70 mm projection. It does not identify models or upgrade dates for individual rooms.

**No qualifying record in this audit:** Ultimate ownership; Property and tenure evidence; Room wheelchair positions; Hearing, visual or cognitive access; Screen/display measurements; Room length × width × height; Room floor area; Seating specification.

## Mooby Bosque

**Directory:** current entry. **Address:** MOOBY Bosque, Rambla de Prat, 16, Gràcia, 08012 Barcelona.

[Official website](https://www.moobycinemas.com/bosque) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mooby%20Bosque%20Barcelona) [Google Maps — MOOBY Bosque](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mooby%20Bosque%20Barcelona)

Verified Google place ID: ChIJg_RkeaKipBIRL2cu-hiaY3o. Coordinates: 41.401491799999995, 2.1518736.

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Mooby Cinemas (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **Group:** Grup Balañá (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **Declared website company:** Edificios y Espectáculos, S.L. · CIF B08176430 (documented; Company behind Mooby website/brand; venue-specific leaseholding entity not established) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Ultimate beneficial ownership:** Not established. 
- **Venue associated project applicant:** Cine Bosque, S.L. · NIF B08335549 (documented; Applicant for this venue’s thermal-energy project; property ownership and lease not established.) [Mooby Bosque thermal installation project report](https://www.moobycinemas.com/assets/Informe-projectes-mes-100-kw-bosque.pdf)
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **1905-03-19:** Teatre del Bosc opened as a performance venue. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **1906-10:** First films screened at the venue. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **1917-06-21:** Replacement theatre building opened. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **1930-12-25:** Sound-film presentation introduced. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **1965-12-22:** Rebuilt cinema opened under Pere Balañá with El Padrecito. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **1996:** Closed for redevelopment. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **1998-08-14:** Reopened as a multiplex. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **2023:** Circuit cinema activities adopted the Mooby Cinemas brand. (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **Architecture:** Façade retains four Pau Gargallo portrait heads: Picasso, Nonell, Reventós and Gargallo. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)
- **Architecture:** {'width': 23, 'depth': 31, 'height': 11, 'unit': 'm'} (documented; Historical 1965 building block, NOT dimensions of any present auditorium) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català — Bosque history](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cine-bosque-barcelona)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com (documented; Circuit-wide contact) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Contact:** Carrer de Provença 266, 5º 2ª, 08008 Barcelona (documented; Company registered/contact address; not cinema entrance) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Contact:** +34 932 159 570 (documented; Public Google Maps business telephone; not independently confirmed) [Google Maps — MOOBY Bosque](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mooby%20Bosque%20Barcelona)
- **Facilities:** Nine rooms; Sala 1 is advertised with 4K and Dolby Atmos. (documented) [Mooby: about Bosque](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-bosque)
- **Access / transport:** Apropa Cultura provides a venue accessibility listing; detailed current provision should be checked there. (documented) [Apropa Cultura: Mooby Bosque interior](https://www.apropacultura.org/es/equipamiento/mooby-bosque)
- **Accessibility:** Mooby says its cinemas support reduced-mobility access and asks wheelchair users to purchase at the cinema box office. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Booking:** Mobile tickets are accepted. Published policy allows no ticket changes or refunds. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Hours:** Cinemas open 15 minutes before the first screening. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com; booking-process support chat Monday–Friday 15:00–20:00, Saturday/Sunday 12:00–20:00. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Private hire:** Private screenings and event hire are offered through the operator’s venue-hire enquiry form. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Parking:** The operator lists parking within the cinema complex; tariff and validation remain unverified. (published) [Mooby: about Bosque](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-bosque)
- **Transport:** Nearby Fontana is on metro L3. (station_verified_nearby_location_inference; Walking time and a step-free journey have not been measured.) [TMB metro station and line information](https://www.tmb.cat/en/barcelona-transport/convenience-services-metro-stations)
- **Accessibility:** Apropa Cultura lists an accessible entrance, adapted lifts, accessible toilets and parking, wheelchair spaces, guide-dog access and easy-read information. (published; Venue listing; no per-room wheelchair inventory or hearing-system specification.) [Apropa Cultura: Mooby Bosque interior](https://www.apropacultura.org/es/equipamiento/mooby-bosque)
- **Company:** Cine Bosque, S.L. (NIF B08335549) is named as applicant for the thermal-energy project at Rambla de Prat 16. (published; Documented venue-associated company, not proof of property ownership or the current lease.) [Mooby Bosque thermal installation project report](https://www.moobycinemas.com/assets/Informe-projectes-mes-100-kw-bosque.pdf)
- **Equipment:** Published aerothermal project: 725.6 kW; report lists Midea MV8i-560WV2RN1E(PRO), MV8i-450WV2RN1E(PRO) and CIAT VECTIOS IPJ 0240CTR, 0180CTR and 0160CTR equipment. (project_specification; Heating/cooling project, not projection equipment. Commissioning and final installed configuration are not established.) ## Mooby Aribau

**Directory:** current entry. **Address:** MOOBY Aribau - Aribau Cinema, Carrer d'Aribau, 8, 10, Eixample, 08011 Barcelona.

[Official website](https://www.moobycinemas.com/aribau) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mooby%20Aribau%20Barcelona) [Google Maps — MOOBY Aribau - Aribau Cinema](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mooby%20Aribau%20Barcelona)

Verified Google place ID: ChIJ74nVg4yipBIRxNcmb8HE_Ng. Coordinates: 41.386125, 2.1625929999999998.

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Mooby Cinemas (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **Group:** Grup Balañá (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **Declared website company:** Edificios y Espectáculos, S.L. · CIF B08176430 (documented; Company behind Mooby website/brand; venue-specific leaseholding entity not established) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Ultimate beneficial ownership:** Not established. 
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **1962-12-07:** Original Aribau Cinema opened with West Side Story. (documented) [Aribau 50th anniversary / opening date](https://www.europapress.es/catalunya/noticia-aribau-cinema-celebra-50-anos-proyeccion-west-side-story-20121207111724.html)
- **2000:** Original large auditorium became part of the five-screen Aribau complex. (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **2023:** Circuit cinema activities adopted the Mooby Cinemas brand. (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **2026-05:** Specialist report records partial seat replacement in Sala 5. (specialist_reported) [Sala Abierta: Aribau Sala 5 seating update](https://www.salabierta.es/?id=155&page_id=2314&view=topic)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com (documented; Circuit-wide contact) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Contact:** Carrer de Provença 266, 5º 2ª, 08008 Barcelona (documented; Company registered/contact address; not cinema entrance) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Facilities:** Five rooms including the large original Aribau Cinema (Sala 5); event and festival use. (documented) [Mooby: about Aribau](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-aribau)
- **Facilities:** Separate operator-linked tours for rooms 1–4 and the large auditorium. (documented) [Mooby: about Aribau](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-aribau)
- **Accessibility:** Mooby says its cinemas support reduced-mobility access and asks wheelchair users to purchase at the cinema box office. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Booking:** Mobile tickets are accepted. Published policy allows no ticket changes or refunds. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Hours:** Cinemas open 15 minutes before the first screening. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com; booking-process support chat Monday–Friday 15:00–20:00, Saturday/Sunday 12:00–20:00. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Private hire:** Private screenings and event hire are offered through the operator’s venue-hire enquiry form. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Transport:** IN-EDIT lists Universitat (L1/L2) and Catalunya (L1/L3); buses 52, 54, H12, V13, 59, 63, 67 and 68 at Gran Via/Balmes or Plaça Universitat. (published; Organizer’s directions, not a live timetable.) [IN-EDIT practical information for Aribau](https://es.in-edit.org/informacion-practica-in-edit/)
- **Accessibility:** Rooms 1–4 have lift access, street-level accessible toilets and two wheelchair positions per room, according to IN-EDIT. (published) [IN-EDIT practical information for Aribau](https://es.in-edit.org/informacion-practica-in-edit/)
- **Accessibility:** Sala 5 uses a platform stairlift with a 300 kg limit. Wheelchair positions are in the aisle; accessible toilets are in rooms 1–4 opposite Sala 5. (published) [IN-EDIT practical information for Aribau](https://es.in-edit.org/informacion-practica-in-edit/)

## Cinesa Diagonal Mar

[Official website](https://www.cinesa.es/Cines/diagonal-mar/) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cinesa%20Diagonal%20Mar%20Barcelona) [Google Maps — CINESA DIAGONAL MAR · ISENSE · IMAX](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cinesa%20Diagonal%20Mar%20Barcelona)

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Cinesa (documented) [ODEON Cinemas Group — brands and AMC parent](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/)
- **Group:** ODEON Cinemas Group → AMC (documented) [ODEON Cinemas Group — brands and AMC parent](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/)
- **Legal company:** Cinesa – Compañía de Iniciativas y Espectáculos, S.A. (documented; Spanish group beneficiary; direct premises title not established) [ODEON — Spanish group beneficiary company](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/wp-content/themes/odeoncinemasgroup/media/filer_public/2e/ad/2ead4992-69c3-4a5d-b451-0c863df1d6b6/odeon_beneficiaries.pdf)
- **Property / tenure:** DWS reported as shopping-centre owner in November 2026; a sale process was reported. Completion/new ownership not verified. (reported_as_of_2026-11-02; Shopping centre, distinct from cinema operator) [Cinco Días — DWS sale process at Diagonal Mar](https://cincodias.elpais.com/companias/2026-11-02/el-fondo-soberano-de-noruega-se-lanza-a-por-el-macrocentro-comercial-de-diagonal-mar-en-barcelona.html)
- **2001-11:** AMC cinema opened with Diagonal Mar shopping centre; contemporary coverage describes 18 screens. (documented) [El País — Diagonal Mar opening, including AMC cinemas](https://elpais.com/diario/2001/11/21/catalunya/1006308454_850215.html)
- **2006:** Cinesa acquired AMC’s Spanish cinema business. (documented) [Cinco Días — Cinesa acquisition of AMC Spain](https://cincodias.elpais.com/cincodias/2006/02/10/empresas/1139582397_850215.html)
- **2019-12-19:** IMAX auditorium launch. (documented) [Audiovisual451 — Barcelona IMAX opening](https://www.audiovisual451.com/cinesa-diagonal-mar-inaugura-la-primera-sala-imax-de-barcelona/)

### Visitor information and services

- **Facilities:** Current room catalogue: 11 rooms; IMAX Sala 9 and iSense Sala 10. (documented) [Cinesa Diagonal Mar — Las salas](https://www.cinesa.es/cines/diagonal-mar/)
- **Facilities:** Mall dining and services; cinema has event-hire listings. (documented) [Diagonal Mar centre cinema page](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/en/cinema/) [VenuesPlace: Cinesa Diagonal Mar room-labelled photos](https://www.venuesplace.com/es/6897-cinesa-diagonal-mar)
- **Access / transport:** Located within Diagonal Mar shopping centre. Check the centre for current access and parking conditions. (documented) [Diagonal Mar centre cinema page](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/en/cinema/)
- **Membership:** Unlimited has a three-month minimum. VIP seats are included where available; annual subscriptions do not receive a refund on cancellation. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa Unlimited minimum term](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9490810498973--Debo-suscribirme-por-un-per%C3%ADodo-m%C3%ADnimo) [Cinesa Unlimited subscription tiers](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9490327230493--Hay-varios-tipos-de-suscripci%C3%B3n)
- **Booking:** Published policy rejects ordinary changes/cancellations, with exceptions for cinema-caused technical failure or cancellation. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa changing or cancelling tickets](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9110970066589--Puedo-cambiar-o-cancelar-mi-entrada-de-cine)
- **Booking:** Registered myCinesa users can change selected seats in the app; this is separate from changing or cancelling a screening. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa changing selected seats](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/17125526586525--Puedo-cambiar-los-asientos-elegidos)
- **Accessibility:** Wheelchair positions are marked in online booking maps. Staff can assist with in-person booking and special assistance. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa customers with reduced mobility](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/7536279675421-Clientes-con-movilidad-reducida) [Cinesa special assistance](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/7536308854429-Asistencia-especial)
- **Transport:** The shopping centre lists El Maresme | Fòrum on L4. (published) [Diagonal Mar terrace and directions](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/la-terrassa-del-mar/)
- **Parking:** Three free parking hours per day, plus one extra hour with the centre app or a cinema ticket presented at the manual cashier on P1 before payment. (published) [Diagonal Mar parking and services](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/servicios/)
- **Parking:** The centre lists 5,000 parking spaces across five underground levels; 54 are designated accessible spaces. (published) [Diagonal Mar parking and services](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/servicios/) [Diagonal Mar accessible parking inventory](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/en/servicios/)
- **Hours:** Winter parking: Monday–Thursday 08:30–01:00; Friday/Saturday/holiday eves 08:30–03:00; Sunday and closed shopping holidays 11:00–01:00. (published; Winter schedule, 1 October–31 May; shopping and cinema opening hours differ.) [Diagonal Mar published opening hours](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/horarios/) [Diagonal Mar parking and services](https://www.diagonalmarcentre.es/servicios/)

### Images, reviews and gaps

**11 image/drawing references** and **26 review observations**, linked by ID in the [combined JSON](cinema-comprehensive-research.json).

- Full review corpus and original review/photo permalinks.
- Exact screen and auditorium dimensions; projector models.
- Current per-performance prices and premium supplements.
- Older 18-room reviews cannot be mapped directly to today’s 11 rooms; published and mapped capacities disagree.
- Published room guide and live seat maps disagree for all 11 rooms. Room 9 wheelchair counts also disagree: guide 4, map 2.
- IMAX and iSense screen sizes and exact projector models remain unverified.

**No qualifying record in this audit:** Ultimate ownership; Architecture; Public contact; Hearing, visual or cognitive access; Screen/display measurements; Room length × width × height; Room floor area.

## Cinesa LUXE La Farga

**Photo provenance follow-up:** Two unconfirmed gallery photographs also appear byte-for-byte in Barnasud listings. Their venue and room attribution remain unresolved. [Duplicate audit](cinema-technical-deep-photos.md).

**Directory:** current entry. **Address:** CINESA LUXE LA FARGA, La Farga, Av. de Josep Tarradellas i Joan, 145, 08901 L'Hospitalet de Llobregat, Barcelona.

[Official website](https://www.cinesa.es/cines/la-farga/) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cinesa%20LUXE%20La%20Farga%20Barcelona) [Google Maps — CINESA LUXE LA FARGA](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cinesa%20LUXE%20La%20Farga%20Barcelona)

Verified Google place ID: ChIJv56Avx-ZpBIR9Sn83R7ry0I. Coordinates: 41.3631793, 2.1047766.

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Cinesa (documented) [ODEON Cinemas Group — brands and AMC parent](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/)
- **Group:** ODEON Cinemas Group → AMC (documented) [ODEON Cinemas Group — brands and AMC parent](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/)
- **Legal company:** Cinesa – Compañía de Iniciativas y Espectáculos, S.A. (documented; Spanish group beneficiary; direct premises title not established) [ODEON — Spanish group beneficiary company](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/wp-content/themes/odeoncinemasgroup/media/filer_public/2e/ad/2ead4992-69c3-4a5d-b451-0c863df1d6b6/odeon_beneficiaries.pdf)
- **Property / tenure:** Inversiones y Promociones GL 2023, S.L.; buyer announcement describes 50/50 participation by LRI and Guillem Capital. (documented; Shopping-centre acquisition announced 10 April 2025; cinema operator remains Cinesa) [López Real Inversiones — La Farga acquisition](https://www.lopezrealinversiones21.com/inversiones-y-promociones-gl-2023-s-l-adquiere-el-centro-comercial-la-farga-barcelona/)
- **1996-04-02:** Max Center / La Farga opened with seven Cinesa screens. (local_history_documented) [Centre d’Estudis de l’Hospitalet — neighbourhood cinema history](https://celh.cat/sites/default/files/publicacions/pdf/9_CINEMES_BARRI.pdf)
- **2019-12:** Converted to Cinesa LUXE. (documented) [Hi Retail — La Farga LUXE conversion and facilities](https://hiretail.es/ocio-cine-y-libros/la-farga-cinesa-luxe-bate-record-de-asistencia/)
- **2025-04-10:** New shopping-centre ownership announced. (documented) [López Real Inversiones — La Farga acquisition](https://www.lopezrealinversiones21.com/inversiones-y-promociones-gl-2023-s-l-adquiere-el-centro-comercial-la-farga-barcelona/)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** gerencia@centrolafarga.com / +34 933 380 737 (documented; Shopping-centre management, not cinema customer service) [La Farga — cinema and parking information](https://centrolafarga.com/cines/)
- **Contact:** parking@centrolafarga.com (documented; Parking enquiries) [La Farga — cinema and parking information](https://centrolafarga.com/cines/)
- **Facilities:** Seven LUXE rooms; reclining seats and individual tables. All seven operator-listed with 4K projection and Dolby 7.1. (documented) [Cinesa LUXE La Farga — Las salas](https://www.cinesa.es/cines/la-farga/) [Hi Retail — La Farga LUXE conversion and facilities](https://hiretail.es/ocio-cine-y-libros/la-farga-cinesa-luxe-bate-record-de-asistencia/)
- **Access / transport:** Centre advertises three hours of free parking during a Cinesa film visit; confirm validation at the venue. (documented) [La Farga — cinema and parking information](https://centrolafarga.com/cines/)
- **Membership:** Unlimited has a three-month minimum. VIP seats are included where available; annual subscriptions do not receive a refund on cancellation. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa Unlimited minimum term](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9490810498973--Debo-suscribirme-por-un-per%C3%ADodo-m%C3%ADnimo) [Cinesa Unlimited subscription tiers](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9490327230493--Hay-varios-tipos-de-suscripci%C3%B3n)
- **Booking:** Published policy rejects ordinary changes/cancellations, with exceptions for cinema-caused technical failure or cancellation. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa changing or cancelling tickets](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9110970066589--Puedo-cambiar-o-cancelar-mi-entrada-de-cine)
- **Booking:** Registered myCinesa users can change selected seats in the app; this is separate from changing or cancelling a screening. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa changing selected seats](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/17125526586525--Puedo-cambiar-los-asientos-elegidos)
- **Accessibility:** Wheelchair positions are marked in online booking maps. Staff can assist with in-person booking and special assistance. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa customers with reduced mobility](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/7536279675421-Clientes-con-movilidad-reducida) [Cinesa special assistance](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/7536308854429-Asistencia-especial)
- **Transport:** Centre entrances: Calle de Barcelona 2 and Avenida Josep Tarradellas s/n. Published public transport: Rambla Just Oliveras metro, Hospitalet de Llobregat commuter rail, Sant Josep FGC, buses L12/LH2. (published) [La Farga access, transport and parking tariff](https://centrolafarga.com/informacion-y-contacto/)
- **Parking:** Three hours of free parking are advertised while watching a film at Cinesa. The page does not explain the validation procedure. (published) [La Farga access, transport and parking tariff](https://centrolafarga.com/informacion-y-contacto/)
- **Hours:** Parking opens Monday–Thursday/Sunday 06:00–01:00 and Friday/Saturday/holiday eves 06:00–03:30. (published) [La Farga access, transport and parking tariff](https://centrolafarga.com/informacion-y-contacto/)
- **Contact:** Parking enquiries: parking@centrolafarga.com; centre management: +34 933 380 737. (published; Shopping centre, not cinema box office.) [La Farga access, transport and parking tariff](https://centrolafarga.com/informacion-y-contacto/)

### Images, reviews and gaps

**11 image/drawing references** and **27 review observations**, linked by ID in the [combined JSON](cinema-comprehensive-research.json).

- Full review corpus and original review/photo permalinks.
- Exact screen and auditorium dimensions; projector models.
- Current per-performance prices and premium supplements.
- Older La Farga 3D mirror reviews may describe the pre-2019 cinema; they are not evidence of current recliner condition.
- All seven rooms are operator-listed as 4K / Dolby 7.1 / LUXE. No room-level Atmos or iSense claim found.
- Published butacas match total mapped positions, including wheelchair and companion positions. Do not add the separate wheelchair figures again.

**No qualifying record in this audit:** Ultimate ownership; Architecture; Hearing, visual or cognitive access; Private hire and education; Screen/display measurements; Room length × width × height; Room floor area.

<a id="yelmo-maquinista"></a>
## Yelmo Premium La Maquinista

**Directory:** current entry. **Address:** Cine Yelmo Westfield La Maquinista, Westfield La Maquinista, Carrer de Potosí, 2, Sant Andreu, 08030 Barcelona.

[Official website](https://yelmocines.es/cartelera/barcelona/westfield-la-maquinista) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Yelmo%20Premium%20La%20Maquinista%20Barcelona) [Google Maps — Cine Yelmo Westfield La Maquinista](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Yelmo%20Premium%20La%20Maquinista%20Barcelona)

Verified Google place ID: ChIJvzQ5Neq8pBIRfUoQLf4vaD4. Coordinates: 41.4395825, 2.1983539.

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Cine Yelmo (documented) [Cine&Tele — Yelmo takes over La Maquinista](https://www.cineytele.com/cine-yelmo-gestionara-las-salas-de-westfield-la-maquinista/)
- **Parent:** Cinépolis; acquired Yelmo in 2015. (documented) [CNMC — Yelmo / La Maquinista concentration report](https://www.cnmc.es/sites/default/files/4583851.pdf)
- **Venue specific legal entity:** Not established. 
- **Declared website company:** YELMO FILMS, S.L.U. · NIF B82721473 (documented; September 2026 website/app legal notice; local leaseholder not established.) [Cine Yelmo legal and purchase conditions September 2026](https://pimcore-content.cinepolis.com/assets/Espa%C3%B1a/PDF/Aviso_Legal_Cine_Yelmo_act%20sep%202026.pdf)
- **Property / tenure:** Unibail-Rodamco-Westfield reports a 51% share in the shopping-centre property in its 2025 report; remaining co-owner not established here. (reported_as_of_2025; Dated shopping-centre ownership disclosure, not cinema company) [URW Universal Registration Document 2025](https://downloads.eu.ctfassets.net/1e76kztii87u/15jfErD57VrebVfSXlboNQ/8b677c7a55e2c06f051a60ca02cc3401/URD_URW_2025_EN_FINAL_VERSION.pdf?L=0&id=163)
- **2000-06:** Warner Lusomundo cinema opened; original complex had 13 rooms. Exact day differs between historical accounts. (documented) [Sala Abierta — Warner La Maquinista history](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=594) [El País — new Warner La Maquinista cinemas](https://elpais.com/diario/2000/06/23/catalunya/961722466_850215.html)
- **2005:** Warner Lusomundo Spanish business passed to Cinesa. (documented) [Sala Abierta — Warner La Maquinista history](https://www.salabierta.es/?p=594)
- **2023-06:** Yelmo took over; management start announced for 15 June. (documented) [Cine&Tele — Yelmo takes over La Maquinista](https://www.cineytele.com/cine-yelmo-gestionara-las-salas-de-westfield-la-maquinista/)
- **2024-10:** Premium refurbishment unveiled. (documented) [Metrópoli — La Maquinista premium refurbishment](https://metropoliabierta.elespanol.com/sant-andreu/20241002/la-maquinista-estrena-sus-renovados-cines-yelmo-novedades-tecnologicas-experiencias-inmersivas/890411053_0.amp.html)

### Visitor information and services

- **Facilities:** 12 rooms: current programme identifies 4D E-Motion in 4, Junior in 7, XE in 8/9 and Atmos in 12. (documented) [Yelmo public programme, all languages](https://yelmocines.es/now-playing.aspx/GetNowPlaying) [Yelmo cinema list distributed by MyEntrada, February 2025](https://www.myentrada.com/imgs/YELMO_CINES-RELACI%C3%93N_2025-02.pdf)
- **Facilities:** Technical feature reports Barco Series IV 4K laser across 12 rooms, Dolby audio/servers, some Barco servers and Screenwriter TMS; Kelonik integration. (documented) [Rubik: La Maquinista technical feature](https://rubik-audiovisual.com/cine-yelmo-premium-westfield-la-maquinista-todo-en-uno/)
- **Access / transport:** Westfield lists cinema on floor 2. Published venue hours: Mon–Thu 15:45–01:00; Fri 15:45–02:00; Sat 12:00–02:00; Sun 12:00–01:00. Hours are a dated listing, not guaranteed performance times. (documented) [Westfield — Cine Yelmo venue listing](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/ocio/cine-yelmo/79034)
- **Company:** Yelmo’s September 2026 notice names YELMO FILMS, S.L.U., NIF B82721473, as the website/app company. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cine Yelmo legal and purchase conditions September 2026](https://pimcore-content.cinepolis.com/assets/Espa%C3%B1a/PDF/Aviso_Legal_Cine_Yelmo_act%20sep%202026.pdf)
- **Pricing conditions:** Yelmo’s terms describe possible Senior (over 60), youth/student (13–30), child (3–13) and disability discounts. Disability eligibility is 33% or more and may include one companion. Categories and amounts are not guaranteed for every cinema/session. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cine Yelmo legal and purchase conditions September 2026](https://pimcore-content.cinepolis.com/assets/Espa%C3%B1a/PDF/Aviso_Legal_Cine_Yelmo_act%20sep%202026.pdf)
- **Admission:** Under-3s may enter without a ticket if they occupy no seat, except Sala Junior, where everyone needs a ticket. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cine Yelmo legal and purchase conditions September 2026](https://pimcore-content.cinepolis.com/assets/Espa%C3%B1a/PDF/Aviso_Legal_Cine_Yelmo_act%20sep%202026.pdf)
- **Booking:** Seat selection is part of purchase; the published conditions allow no changes or refunds after confirmation. Prices include VAT and may vary by date, time and format. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cine Yelmo legal and purchase conditions September 2026](https://pimcore-content.cinepolis.com/assets/Espa%C3%B1a/PDF/Aviso_Legal_Cine_Yelmo_act%20sep%202026.pdf)
- **Membership:** The terms mention MovieYELMO, with at most two tickets per web transaction and film/promotion exclusions. No current membership tariff was established. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cine Yelmo legal and purchase conditions September 2026](https://pimcore-content.cinepolis.com/assets/Espa%C3%B1a/PDF/Aviso_Legal_Cine_Yelmo_act%20sep%202026.pdf)
- **Transport:** The centre lists L1 Sant Andreu/Torras i Bages and L9/L10 Bon Pastor. Underground parking entrances are on Potosí and São Paulo; exterior parking is reached from República Dominicana. (published; The L9/L10 directions refer to the northern branches; no walking time is asserted.) [Westfield La Maquinista access](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/acceso)
- **Parking:** 4,800 spaces across two underground levels and an exterior car park; 2.2 m height limit. First three hours free; Westfield Club adds one hour. A Carrefour purchase over EUR 20 can alternatively add one hour; benefits cannot be combined. (published) [Westfield La Maquinista parking](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/parking)
- **Hours:** Parking: Monday–Thursday/Sunday 08:30–01:30; Friday/Saturday 08:30–03:30. (published) [Westfield La Maquinista parking](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/parking)
- **Accessibility:** The centre lists accessible circulation/parking and wheelchair rental, plus electric charging and bicycle/motorcycle parking. (published; Shopping-centre facilities; not a room-by-room cinema accessibility audit.) [Westfield La Maquinista services](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/nuestros-servicios) [Westfield La Maquinista parking](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/parking)

Dated amounts and conditions; checkout or the venue controls the current price.

| Product | Amount | Conditions / scope | Source |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Parking hourly rate | 3.25 EUR | parking; Published paid-parking rate after applicable free allowance; tariff panel explicitly labels the 4–24 hour band, so non-member hour 3–4 should be checked.; Published tariff; checkout controls the final price | [Westfield La Maquinista parking](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/parking) |
| Parking daily maximum | 20 EUR | parking; Published daily cap.; Published tariff; checkout controls the final price | [Westfield La Maquinista parking](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/lamaquinista/parking) |

### Images, reviews and gaps

**12 image/drawing references** and **9 review observations**, linked by ID in the [combined JSON](cinema-comprehensive-research.json).

- Venue-specific legal operating entity and shopping-centre co-owner.
- Exact dimensions of both XE screens and all other current screens.
- Original opening day (15 versus 16 June 2000 in secondary accounts).
- Matched current cinema-specific Tripadvisor listing; mall reviews excluded.
- Full review corpus and original review/photo permalinks.
- Current assisted-listening provision and detailed room access routes; parking terms are now linked.
- The 2025 technical feature reports all 12 rooms use Barco Series IV 4K laser; Dolby speakers/servers, some Barco servers, and Arts Alliance Media Screenwriter TMS. Kelonik handled projection/audio.
- All 12 room layouts collected and joined to public schedule plus booking summary. Counts are mapped positions, not certified capacities. Sala 12 has 403 row entries versus a 402-position API area total.
- Current sessions identify XE in both 8 and 9; a February 2025 distributor list also specifies two XE rooms. Exact screen measurements per XE room remain unverified.

**No qualifying record in this audit:** Ultimate ownership; Architecture; Public contact; Hearing, visual or cognitive access; Private hire and education; Screen/display measurements; Room length × width × height; Room floor area.

## Kinépolis Barcelona Splau

**Directory:** current entry. **Address:** KINEPOLIS CINEMAS, Av. del Baix Llobregat, s/n, 08940 Cornellà de Llobregat, Barcelona.

[Official website](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kin%C3%A9polis%20Barcelona%20Splau%20Barcelona) [Google Maps — KINEPOLIS CINEMAS](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kin%C3%A9polis%20Barcelona%20Splau%20Barcelona)

Verified Google place ID: ChIJn13KLEyZpBIRDBdVtKH83bo. Coordinates: 41.347440899999995, 2.0788224.

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Kinépolis (documented) [Kinépolis Splau room information (Spanish)](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/)
- **Parent:** Kinepolis Group (documented) [Kinepolis annual report 2019 — El Punt acquisition](https://corporate.kinepolis.com/sites/default/files/media/13072/KINEP_2045_JVSL19_EN.pdf)
- **Previous group:** El Punt; Sallent family. (documented) [Kinepolis annual report 2019 — El Punt acquisition](https://corporate.kinepolis.com/sites/default/files/media/13072/KINEP_2045_JVSL19_EN.pdf)
- **Venue specific current legal entity:** Not established. 
- **Property / tenure:** Shopping centre held jointly by URW and Castellana Properties, 50% each; Castellana transaction completed 13 May 2026. URW retained management. Cinema premises were reported as leased in Kinepolis’s acquisition filing. (documented; Shopping centre / leased cinema premises) [Castellana — 50% Splau acquisition agreement](https://www.castellanaproperties.es/en/prensa/noticias/134-castellana-properties-acquires-a-50-stake-in) [Castellana — Splau acquisition completion](https://www.castellanaproperties.es/files/uploads/file/91ae8566-4cca-4b0f-ab9a-0c1b44b3a950/260513-oir-adquisicion-splau-es.pdf?qs=1778773757) [Kinepolis annual report 2019 — El Punt acquisition](https://corporate.kinepolis.com/sites/default/files/media/13072/KINEP_2045_JVSL19_EN.pdf)
- **2010-05-11:** Splau shopping centre opened; this is NOT the cinema opening date. (documented) [Distribución Actualidad — Splau first anniversary](https://distribucionactualidad.com/splau-celebra-su-primer-ao-de-vida/)
- **2010-12-13:** Full HD cinema opened with 18 screens; opening report names promoters Pere Sallent and Josep Campreciós. (documented) [José M. Queraltó / Kelonik — Full HD opening](https://coleccionjmqueralto.blogspot.com/2010/12/inaugurados-los-cines-full-hd-en.html)
- **2013-07-10:** Contemporary report records inauguration of ten additional rooms, expanding Full HD from 18 to 28. (contemporary_report) [Full HD ten-screen expansion inaugurated](https://www.lavanguardia.com/local/baix-llobregat/20130710/54377412745/cines-full-hd-cornella-multisalas.html?facet=amp)
- **2019-02-28:** Kinepolis completed acquisition of Full from El Punt; 28 rooms by this date. (documented) [Kinepolis annual report 2019 — El Punt acquisition](https://corporate.kinepolis.com/sites/default/files/media/13072/KINEP_2045_JVSL19_EN.pdf)
- **2019-03-01:** Transfer of operating control to Kinepolis. (documented) [Kinepolis annual report 2019 — El Punt acquisition](https://corporate.kinepolis.com/sites/default/files/media/13072/KINEP_2045_JVSL19_EN.pdf)
- **2026-05-13:** Castellana completed its 50% shopping-centre acquisition. (documented) [Castellana — Splau acquisition completion](https://www.castellanaproperties.es/files/uploads/file/91ae8566-4cca-4b0f-ab9a-0c1b44b3a950/260513-oir-adquisicion-splau-es.pdf?qs=1778773757)

### Visitor information and services

- **Facilities:** 28 rooms; published per-room screen measurements and seats are in the linked catalogue. (documented) [Kinépolis Splau room information (Spanish)](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/)
- **Facilities:** Laser ULTRA offer and corporate-event hire; exact current equipment should be read room by room. (documented) [Kinépolis Laser ULTRA](https://kinepolis.es/movie-formats/laser-ultra) [Kinépolis Barcelona corporate events](https://empresas.kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona/)
- **Pricing conditions:** Reduced tickets: children through age 12 and disabled customers every day; age 65+, youth/student card holders Monday–Thursday excluding holidays/eves. Proof required. Under-3s enter free without occupying a seat; VIP/recliner discounts excluded on weekends/holidays. (published) [Kinépolis Splau room information (Spanish)](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/)
- **Hours:** Operator lists daily cinema hours 10:45–01:00 and box-office hours 10:30–23:00. (published) [Kinépolis Splau room information (Spanish)](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/)
- **Contact:** info.espana@kinepolis.com is the operator’s published customer contact. (published) [Kinépolis Splau room information (Spanish)](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/)
- **Transport:** Operator lists Cornellà Riera FGC, Cornellà Centre metro L5, and T1/T2 tram at Cornellà Centre or Les Aigües. (published; Operator directions; older bus-route numbers were not propagated.) [Kinépolis Splau room information (Spanish)](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/)
- **Parking:** The centre advertises 2,800 free underground spaces over two levels, with PMR, family and larger/smaller bays. Eight EV charging points are paid; entrances are on Carrer Progrés and Carretera del Mig. (published) [Westfield Splau free parking](https://www.westfield.com/es/spain/splau/servicios-club/free-parking)
- **Accessibility:** The operator’s current Spanish room table marks all 28 salas as accessible and lists wheelchair positions for each room. These positions are recorded separately from fixed seats in the room catalogue. (published; Operator claim, not an independent route or hearing-equipment audit.) [Kinépolis Splau room information (Spanish)](https://kinepolis.es/cines/kinepolis-barcelona-splau/info/)
- **Booking:** Published rules conflict: the online-refund FAQ gives a request deadline 30 minutes after the start; the wrong-cinema FAQ says 30 minutes before; general conditions describe fixed-session tickets as non-refundable. Confirm the applicable case with customer support. (conflicting_published_rules) [Kinépolis online ticket refund FAQ](https://kinepolis.es/faq-page/tarifas-y-compra-general/puedo-devolver-mis-entradas-compradas-online/) [Kinépolis tickets purchased for the wrong cinema FAQ](https://kinepolis.es/faq-page/tarifas-y-compra-general/he-comprado-por-error-entradas-para-otro-cine-kinepolis-que-puedo/) [Kinépolis purchase conditions](https://kinepolis.es/condiciones-de-compra/)

### Images, reviews and gaps

**8 image/drawing references** and **12 review observations**, linked by ID in the [combined JSON](cinema-comprehensive-research.json).

- Current local operating legal entity.
- Full review corpus and original review/photo permalinks.
- Room length/width/height; conflicting operator seat totals and Catalan/Spanish room tables.
- Current assisted-listening provision and contradictory refund rules; free parking is documented.
- Spanish room table sums to 2,594 seats; PMR column sums to 45. Neither 2,594 nor 2,639 equals the page introduction’s 2,687. Definitions/currentness unresolved.
- Catalan table differs on room 2 capacity and room 19 screen size. Spanish values are the displayed reference, not a claim that the conflict is resolved.
- Screen axes are inferred; raw source order retained. Screen area, aspect ratio, room length/width/height and viewing angles are not inferred.
- Corporate site advertises stages up to 85 m², without room mapping.
- Historical dossier names Barco 2K/4K laser and Christie 2K/4K xenon at venue level, curved screens and Eyes3Shut 3D in rooms 7, 9, 11, 16, 18, 20, 22, 27, 28. These are not current room-equipment confirmations.

**No qualifying record in this audit:** Ultimate ownership; Architecture; Hearing, visual or cognitive access; Room length × width × height; Room floor area; Seating specification.

<a id="mooby-arenas"></a>
## Mooby Arenas de Barcelona

**Directory:** current entry. **Address:** Gran Via de les Corts Catalanes, 385, Barcelona.

[Official website](https://www.moobycinemas.com/arenas) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mooby%20Arenas%20de%20Barcelona%20Gran%20Via%20de%20les%20Corts%20Catalanes%2C%20385%2C%20Barcelona) [Expansion inventory and operator programme verification](barcelona-cinema-inventory.json)

Maps link is a name/address search unless the location record states otherwise; exact Google place identity is unverified.

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Mooby Cinemas (documented) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Group:** Grup Balañá (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **Declared website company:** Edificios y Espectáculos, S.L. · CIF B08176430 (documented; Website legal entity; not proof of premises ownership) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **2011:** Cinema opened under its earlier Multicines name. (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com (documented; Central customer service) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Facilities:** 12 rooms; venue advertises 3D projection. (documented) [Mooby — Mooby Arenas de Barcelona](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-arenas)
- **Access / transport:** Three hours of free parking with a cinema ticket. (documented) [Mooby — Mooby Arenas de Barcelona](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-arenas)
- **Published policy:** Electric scooters and electric unicycles are prohibited by the venue. (documented) [Mooby — Mooby Arenas de Barcelona](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-arenas)
- **Accessibility:** Mooby says its cinemas support reduced-mobility access and asks wheelchair users to purchase at the cinema box office. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Booking:** Mobile tickets are accepted. Published policy allows no ticket changes or refunds. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Hours:** Cinemas open 15 minutes before the first screening. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com; booking-process support chat Monday–Friday 15:00–20:00, Saturday/Sunday 12:00–20:00. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Private hire:** Private screenings and event hire are offered through the operator’s venue-hire enquiry form. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Mooby Cinemas (documented) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Group:** Grup Balañá (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **Declared website company:** Edificios y Espectáculos, S.L. · CIF B08176430 (documented; Website legal entity; not proof of premises ownership) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **1995-05-05:** Cinema opened under its earlier Multicines name. (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)
- **2013:** Renovated and expanded from seven to eight rooms. (documented) [Mooby — company and cinema chronology](https://www.moobycinemas.com/quienes-somos)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com (documented; Central customer service) [Mooby — terms and legal company](https://www.moobycinemas.com/termes-i-condicions)
- **Facilities:** 8 rooms; venue advertises 3D projection. (documented) [Mooby — Mooby Glòries](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-glories)
- **Access / transport:** Operator advertises two hours of free parking; current mall conditions were not independently reconciled. (documented) [Mooby — Mooby Glòries](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-glories)
- **Published policy:** Electric scooters and electric unicycles are prohibited by the venue. (documented) [Mooby — Mooby Glòries](https://www.moobycinemas.com/sobre-glories)
- **Accessibility:** Mooby says its cinemas support reduced-mobility access and asks wheelchair users to purchase at the cinema box office. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Booking:** Mobile tickets are accepted. Published policy allows no ticket changes or refunds. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Hours:** Cinemas open 15 minutes before the first screening. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Contact:** info@moobycinemas.com; booking-process support chat Monday–Friday 15:00–20:00, Saturday/Sunday 12:00–20:00. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)
- **Private hire:** Private screenings and event hire are offered through the operator’s venue-hire enquiry form. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Mooby customer service and booking FAQ](https://www.moobycinemas.com/atencio-al-client)

[Official website](https://www.cinesa.es/cines/diagonal/) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cinesa%20Diagonal%20Santa%20Fe%20de%20Nou%20M%C3%A8xic%2C%20s/n%20%28municipal%20listing%3A%2018%29%2C%20Barcelona) [Expansion inventory and operator programme verification](barcelona-cinema-inventory.json)

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** Cinesa (documented) [ODEON Cinemas Group — brands and AMC parent](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/)
- **Group:** ODEON Cinemas Group → AMC (documented) [ODEON Cinemas Group — brands and AMC parent](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/)
- **Legal company:** Cinesa – Compañía de Iniciativas y Espectáculos, S.A. (documented; Spanish group beneficiary; direct premises title not established) [ODEON — Spanish group beneficiary company](https://www.odeoncinemasgroup.com/wp-content/themes/odeoncinemasgroup/media/filer_public/2e/ad/2ead4992-69c3-4a5d-b451-0c863df1d6b6/odeon_beneficiaries.pdf)
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **1999-07-01:** Contemporary report announced opening that evening with 11 salas and 2,509 seats across the complex. These are launch figures, not a current room inventory. (documented) [El País — Cinesa Diagonal opening, 1 July 1999](https://elpais.com/diario/1999/07/01/catalunya/930791255_850215.html?outputType=amp)

### Visitor information and services

- **Membership:** Unlimited has a three-month minimum. VIP seats are included where available; annual subscriptions do not receive a refund on cancellation. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa Unlimited minimum term](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9490810498973--Debo-suscribirme-por-un-per%C3%ADodo-m%C3%ADnimo) [Cinesa Unlimited subscription tiers](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9490327230493--Hay-varios-tipos-de-suscripci%C3%B3n)
- **Booking:** Published policy rejects ordinary changes/cancellations, with exceptions for cinema-caused technical failure or cancellation. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa changing or cancelling tickets](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/9110970066589--Puedo-cambiar-o-cancelar-mi-entrada-de-cine)
- **Booking:** Registered myCinesa users can change selected seats in the app; this is separate from changing or cancelling a screening. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa changing selected seats](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/17125526586525--Puedo-cambiar-los-asientos-elegidos)
- **Accessibility:** Wheelchair positions are marked in online booking maps. Staff can assist with in-person booking and special assistance. (published; chain_policy_not_room_specific) [Cinesa customers with reduced mobility](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/7536279675421-Clientes-con-movilidad-reducida) [Cinesa special assistance](https://ayuda.cinesa.es/hc/es/articles/7536308854429-Asistencia-especial)

Declared website company; property title and ultimate shareholders are not established. (documented; two_venue_operator_website) [Cines Verdi — legal notice](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/terminos-y-condiciones)
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **1995:** Verdi Park opened in 1995; this is separate from the original Verdi cinema. (documented) [Catalunya tourism — Verdi Park](https://www.catalunya.com/es/continguts/ens-public-privats/verdi-park-17-16009-113)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** Public enquiries: info@cines-verdi.com. (documented; venue) [Cines Verdi — legal notice](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/terminos-y-condiciones)
- **Facilities:** Original-version programme; two separate entrances for Verdi and Verdi Park, with no internal connection. (documented) [Verdi — frequently asked questions](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/preguntas-frecuentes)
- **Access / transport:** Metro Fontana L3 / Joanic L4; FGC Gràcia. Check current service before travelling. (documented) [Verdi — frequently asked questions](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/preguntas-frecuentes)
- **Access / transport:** Operator says most rooms have adapted routes and wheelchair positions; it does not identify every accessible sala. Contact the box office before visiting if assistance is needed. (documented; venue) [Verdi — frequently asked questions](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/preguntas-frecuentes)
- **Access / transport:** Nearby paid options listed: NN Gràcia, Torrent de l’Olla 220, and Bruniquer. No cinema validation or free period established. (documented; venue) [Verdi — frequently asked questions](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/preguntas-frecuentes)
- **Published policy:** PDF/QR tickets can be shown on a phone; printing is unnecessary. (documented; venue) [Verdi — frequently asked questions](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/preguntas-frecuentes)
- **Published policy:** Amigos de los Verdi, senior, youth-card and student discounts are listed; eligibility and session price must be checked. (documented; venue) [Verdi — frequently asked questions](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/preguntas-frecuentes)
- **Published policy:** Bono Cultural Joven accepted at the box office and online. (documented; venue) [Verdi — frequently asked questions](https://barcelona.cines-verdi.com/preguntas-frecuentes)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** info@espaitexas.cat; cinema hire: cinemes@espaitexas.cat (documented) [Espai Texas — room hire](https://espaitexas.cat/lloguer-espai-texas/)

- **Access / transport:** Cinema rooms are in basement -1. Stairlift limit is 150 kg including person and wheelchair; operator states no alternative route. Adapted toilet and bar are on street level. (documented) [Espai Texas — practical information](https://espaitexas.cat/informacio-practica/)
- **Access / transport:** The basement cinemas use a stairlift rated to 150 kg for the person and wheelchair, with no alternative route above that limit. The theatre has a separate alternative entrance. The hall/bar and adapted toilet are at street level. (documented; venue) [Espai Texas — practical information](https://espaitexas.cat/informacio-practica/)
- **Published policy:** Cinema box office opens 30 minutes before first screening. Theatre access/box-office rules are separate. (documented) [Espai Texas — practical information](https://espaitexas.cat/informacio-practica/)

- **Facilities:** Single 73-seat cinema and bistrot; private hire and DCP creation available. (documented) [Zumzeig — room hire and equipment](https://zumzeigcine.coop/es/static/alquiler/)
- **Access / transport:** Adapted entrance/toilet; guide dogs accepted with documentation. No hearing loop. Wheelchair accommodation described at row 7 seat 3 and row 1 seats 1, 2, 8, 9, 10; these are configuration locations, not six additional permanent places. (documented) [Zumzeig — physical accessibility](https://zumzeigcine.coop/mes-informacio/accessibilitat-sala/)
- **Access / transport:** One building entrance and the cinema entrance are adapted; an accessible toilet is available. (documented; venue) [Zumzeig — auditorium accessibility](https://zumzeigcine.coop/es/mas-informacion/accesibilidad-sala/)
- **Access / transport:** Wheelchair accommodation is listed at row 7 seat 3 and row 1 seats 1, 2, 8, 9 and 10. These are published locations, not six seats to add to the 73-seat total. (documented; venue) [Zumzeig — auditorium accessibility](https://zumzeigcine.coop/es/mas-informacion/accesibilidad-sala/)
- **Access / transport:** The operator explicitly states that there is no induction loop. (documented; venue) [Zumzeig — auditorium accessibility](https://zumzeigcine.coop/es/mas-informacion/accesibilidad-sala/)
- **Access / transport:** Films with accessible subtitles are identified individually in their programme entry; availability is not universal. (documented; venue) [Zumzeig — auditorium accessibility](https://zumzeigcine.coop/es/mas-informacion/accesibilidad-sala/)
- **Access / transport:** Documented guide dogs are admitted; drinking bowls are available. Door/furniture contrast is limited in some areas. (documented; venue) [Zumzeig — auditorium accessibility](https://zumzeigcine.coop/es/mas-informacion/accesibilidad-sala/)

- **2005-11:** Reopened as CineBaix under association management. (documented) [CineBaix official history](https://www.cinebaix.cat/la-nostra-historia)
- **1967:** Predecessor Cinema Guinart constructed; architect Miquel Ponseti Vives. (documented) [Sant Feliu municipal CineBaix refurbishment brief, file PACC2021000039](https://contractaciopublica.cat/portal-api/descarrega-document-antic/88592343/be5f16c9f25b8814d5914a2a64321d59)

- **1996:** Upper auditorium subdivided, bringing the cinema to six rooms. (documented) [Sant Feliu municipal CineBaix refurbishment brief, file PACC2021000039](https://contractaciopublica.cat/portal-api/descarrega-document-antic/88592343/be5f16c9f25b8814d5914a2a64321d59)

- **1916-03-28:** Opened as Salón Cataluña; architect Melcior Vinyals, promoter Pere Romaní i Recasens. (documented) [Cinema Catalunya official chronology](https://cinemacatalunya.cat/historia/)
- **2000-03-16:** Reopened after municipal acquisition and refurbishment. (documented) [Cinema Catalunya official chronology](https://cinemacatalunya.cat/historia/)
- **2014-11-14:** Digital conversion commemorated; one analogue projector retained. (documented) [Cinema Catalunya official chronology](https://cinemacatalunya.cat/historia/)

- **Facilities:** Conference/video-projection and satellite-link equipment; room hire available. (documented) [Cinema Catalunya official equipment and capacities](https://cinemacatalunya.cat/cinema-catalunya/)

### Operator, ownership and history

This is a secondary business-directory association, not a verified operating licence or property title. (secondary_company_association; venue) [Iberinform — Alcazar Cinema S.A. company listing](https://www.iberinform.es/empresa/2267006/alcazar-cinema)
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **1911:** Gran Saló Imperial opened, designed by Jeroni Martorell. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català: Imperial history and archive](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cinemes-imperial-sabadell)
- **2000:** Historic building demolished and rebuilt; current multiplex has a replica façade. (documented) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català: Imperial history and archive](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cinemes-imperial-sabadell)
- **2003-12-18:** Local history dates the new multiplex inauguration to 18 December 2003, with 11 rooms and 1,800 seats at launch; the predecessor cinema opened in 1911. (reported_historical) [iSabadell — inauguration of the Imperial multiplex](https://www.isabadell.cat/sabadell/historia/inauguracio-multisales-imperial-2003-250557/)
- **Architecture:** Original auditorium was 42 m long and held roughly 1,000 people. These are historical dimensions/capacity, not current sala data. (historical) [Acadèmia del Cinema Català: Imperial history and archive](https://academiadelcinema.cat/ca/cinemes-imperial-sabadell)

## CCCB

**Directory:** current entry. **Address:** Carrer de Montalegre, 5, 08001 Barcelona.

[Official website](https://www.cccb.org/) · [Google Maps](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=CCCB%20Carrer%20de%20Montalegre%2C%205%2C%2008001%20Barcelona) [L’Alternativa 2026 official venues](https://alternativa.cccb.org/2026/ca/festival/informacio/seus)

Maps link is a name/address search unless the location record states otherwise; exact Google place identity is unverified.

### Operator, ownership and history

- **Brand:** CCCB (documented) [L’Alternativa 2026 official venues](https://alternativa.cccb.org/2026/ca/festival/informacio/seus)
- **Legal company:** Consorci Centre de Cultura Contemporània de Barcelona – Casa de Caritat (documented) [CCCB organisation and statutes](https://www.cccb.org/ca/sobre-nosaltres/organitzacio-estatuts)
- **Property / tenure:** Not established. (not_established) 
- **1994:** CCCB opened. (documented) [CCCB institutional overview](https://www.cccb.org/ca/sobre-nosaltres)

### Visitor information and services

- **Contact:** 93 306 41 00 (documented) [CCCB Sala Teatre technical sheet, January 2026](https://www.cccb.org/documents/4284035/4315836/CCCB_SalaTeatre-equipament_tecnic.pdf/4367aab7-db20-48c3-d22f-cd5ff1e6324d?t=1769759779623)
- **Facilities:** Local consortium funded annually 75% by Diputació de Barcelona and 25% by Ajuntament de Barcelona; these percentages describe operating contributions, not property title. (documented) [CCCB organisation and statutes](https://www.cccb.org/ca/sobre-nosaltres/organitzacio-estatuts)
- **Access / transport:** Ramps and lifts connect the venue. The entrance ramp has a 14% gradient; some routes require staff assistance. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
- **Access / transport:** Adapted toilets on floors −2, 3 and 4; two loan wheelchairs can be reserved. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
- **Access / transport:** Two designated accessible parking bays are listed in front of the Montalegre entrance. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
- **Access / transport:** Auditori has two reserved wheelchair places. Other rooms are described as flat-floor chair layouts; no fixed wheelchair-space count is supplied for them. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
- **Access / transport:** Portable induction loops can be requested at the ticket desk, preferably reserved with the booking; compatible hearing aids need the T setting. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
- **Access / transport:** Catalan sign-language interpretation is available for selected activities or by request; accessible subtitles depend on the event. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
- **Access / transport:** Guide dogs admitted with documentation. Braille, large-print and audiovisual exhibition resources should not be assumed to apply to every screening. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
- **Access / transport:** Reception/ticket counters are reported at 1.10–1.20 m high, without a lowered accessible section. (documented; venue) [CCCB — visitor accessibility](https://www.cccb.org/ca/visita/accessibilitat)
